# ServiceLoop — Individual UI/UX Action Coverage

**Version 0.1 · 5 September 2026 · Integral appendix to the conceptual UI/UX proposal**

[Open the main design](ServiceLoop_Conceptual_UI_UX_Design_v0_1.md).

**How to use this appendix.** Each original action has one individually anchored row. Read its **UI location**, **applicable state/pattern**, and **disposition**, then the preserved original contract. The original contract is evidence of what that source proposed, **not an instruction to activate an explicitly conflicting F behavior**. C remains provisional, with X-01–X-11 and FC-01–FC-03 awaiting owner review. All grouped action labels originate in the source; no unenumerated ID ranges stand in for missing rows.

The main catalogue describes component composition, initial viewport, input order, saving, states, resilience and rationale. These rows supply the exact control home, including overflow, nested editor, system handoff and guarded variants. Repeated patterns are instantiated with each action's actual subject and state. A correspondence to C is semantic/presentation traceability, not a claim that identically numbered screens are equivalent.

**Coverage totals:** 357 C + 375 F = **732 individual actions**. C has 40 destination parents and 13 shared-dialog parents. F has 38 destination parents, 8 shared-pattern parents and 2 embedded-editor parents. Every source parent is represented below. No app implementation, external artwork or additional functional system is provided.

**Source files:** [C Markdown](ServiceLoop_Conceptual_App_Map_v0_1.md) · [F Markdown](ServiceLoop_Complete_Functional_App_Map_v0_1.md). Original documents are included unchanged in the offline bundle. The main design's A/J registers govern conflicts, validation and owner decisions.


<a id="coverage-C"></a>

# C — original action family

## C:D01 · 4 actions

Represented on UI-41. Each row retains its own state/disposition.

| Source action / control | Specific UI location | Applicable state and pattern | Disposition / correspondence | Original source effect and cancellation/failure contract |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="C-D01-A01"></a>**C:D01-A01**<br>Keep editing / Resume recovered input | **UI-41**<br>Decision action Keep editing / Resume recovered input; primary return to the invoking field. | Ordinary dirty/recovered editor, or failed working write; labels depend on actual persistence state.<br>Pattern: P-02 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Return to the same editor and recovered values. On launch, reopen its recorded context.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No business mutation; missing parent produces a safe parent-list explanation. |
| <a id="C-D01-A02"></a>**C:D01-A02**<br>Discard changes | **UI-41**<br>Decision secondary Discard changes; loss summary above, return to committed parent. | Ordinary dirty/recovered editor, or failed working write; labels depend on actual persistence state.<br>Pattern: P-02 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Discard only the uncommitted buffer and return to caller. Existing saved entity is unchanged.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Confirm shows the relevant entity; failed cleanup does not apply the draft. |
| <a id="C-D01-A03"></a>**C:D01-A03**<br>Retry saving | **UI-41**<br>Failed-save banner primary Retry saving; remain with in-memory input. | Ordinary dirty/recovered editor, or failed working write; labels depend on actual persistence state.<br>Pattern: P-02 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Failed durable Working/correction save; retry, then allow departure after acknowledgement.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Remain on failed state if storage is still unavailable. |
| <a id="C-D01-A04"></a>**C:D01-A04**<br>Leave without unsaved changes | **UI-41**<br>Loss-review destructive Leave without unsaved changes, after explicit loss acknowledgement. | Failed saving with a usable earlier durable state; never implies unsaved input was saved.<br>Pattern: P-02 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Only after a save failure; explicitly warn that changes after the displayed checkpoint may be lost. Saved work remains.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Never labelled Cancel visit. |

| <a id="C-D02-A01"></a>**C:D02-A01**<br>Search / Clear search | **UI-42**<br>Top search input and Clear search trailing text/icon. | Context selector for the named entity; eligible rows only, with explicit inactive/historical mode.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Filter the eligible choices by name/reference/identifier; no data mutation.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No matches offers Clear search, not automatic creation. |
| <a id="C-D02-A02"></a>**C:D02-A02**<br>Select row / selection checkbox | **UI-42**<br>Full-row Select for single choice; independent checkbox for multiple choice, full identity visible. | Context selector for the named entity; eligible rows only, with explicit inactive/historical mode.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Stage single or multiple selections in the caller's context.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Ineligible row explains its blocker. |
| <a id="C-D02-A03"></a>**C:D02-A03**<br>Use selection | **UI-42**<br>Bottom Use selection button with selected count/context; not enabled for invalid selection. | Context selector for the named entity; eligible rows only, with explicit inactive/historical mode.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Return staged choices; required count and one-site checks apply.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Disabled until valid; caller still controls its Save. |
| <a id="C-D02-A04"></a>**C:D02-A04**<br>Add customer / Add site / Add equipment / Add template | **UI-42**<br>Below results Add customer / Add site / Add equipment / Add template as applicable, returning saved entity to selector. | Context selector for the named entity; eligible rows only, with explicit inactive/historical mode.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Open the corresponding editor S07/S09/S11/S17 when that type is allowed.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Cancelling returns without a new selection. |
| <a id="C-D02-A05"></a>**C:D02-A05**<br>Show historical/inactive | **UI-42**<br>Filter region Show historical/inactive switch with explanatory scope label. | Only when calling task allows inactive/historical records; never silently makes them live eligible.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Only historical-entry and history-linking modes; current booking never gains eligibility from this switch.<br>**Source continuation / error:** View-only until a permitted historical selection is made. |
| <a id="C-D02-A06"></a>**C:D02-A06**<br>Cancel | **UI-42**<br>Toolbar Cancel and native Back, preserving original parent selection. | Context selector for the named entity; eligible rows only, with explicit inactive/historical mode.<br>Pattern: P-04 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Discard staged selections.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Leaves caller's previous selection unchanged. |

Failed write leaves all related records unchanged. |
| <a id="C-D03-A03"></a>**C:D03-A03**<br>Cancel | **UI-43**<br>Toolbar Cancel / decision secondary Cancel; no mutation. | Named lifecycle/removal instance in the UI-43 matrix; source-specific reason, blockers and effect summary.<br>Pattern: P-09 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Return unchanged.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Always available before commit. |

| <a id="C-D05-A04"></a>**C:D05-A04**<br>Compose email | **UI-45**<br>Email row Compose email button and prepared subject/body preview. | Contact sheet/full page for known customer/site; each target requires an available value.<br>Pattern: P-08 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Open email composer with recipient, subject, and minimal editable body.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No mail account/handler offers Copy details. |
| <a id="C-D05-A05"></a>**C:D05-A05**<br>Open map | **UI-45**<br>Address row Open map button; no in-app navigation subsystem. | Contact sheet/full page for known customer/site; each target requires an available value.<br>Pattern: P-08 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Send the visible site address to a suitable map app.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No route computation or claim that the address was geocoded correctly; missing address offers Edit site. |
| <a id="C-D05-A06"></a>**C:D05-A06**<br>Copy phone / email / address / prepared message | **UI-45**<br>Each value/message trailing Copy phone / Copy email / Copy address / Copy prepared message, individually labelled. | Contact sheet/full page for known customer/site; each target requires an available value.<br>Pattern: P-08 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Copy only the selected visible content after a deliberate tap; show Copied.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Explain clipboard is outside the private record boundary. |
| <a id="C-D05-A07"></a>**C:D05-A07**<br>Record contact outcome | **UI-45**<br>Bottom Record contact outcome opens UI-29 after handoff or directly; not a successful default. | Contact sheet/full page for known customer/site; each target requires an available value.<br>Pattern: P-08 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Open S29; technician supplies what actually happened.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Returning from another app merely offers this action, never presses it automatically. |
| <a id="C-D05-A08"></a>**C:D05-A08**<br>Close | **UI-45**<br>Toolbar Close and native Back return to original context. | Contact sheet/full page for known customer/site; each target requires an available value.<br>Pattern: P-08 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Return to context.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No changed obligation, booking, or follow-up. |

Blockers open D03. |
| <a id="C-D06-A02"></a>**C:D06-A02**<br>Keep current value | **UI-46**<br>Choice beside proposed date Keep current value. | Applicable edit/resume/reconciliation where source allows retaining current value; not an excuse to skip required work.<br>Pattern: P-09 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** On resume/date suggestions, retain the old due date explicitly, even when overdue.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No invisible recalculation. |

| <a id="C-D07-A03"></a>**C:D07-A03**<br>Reopen | **UI-47**<br>Closed-task action Reopen, source-valid date/reason context and Confirm reopen. | Follow-up state review for one identified saved task.<br>Pattern: P-09 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Required reason and new follow-up date; preserve previous closure entries.<br>**Source continuation / error:** Active context required; otherwise explain restore/move prerequisites. |
| <a id="C-D07-A04"></a>**C:D07-A04**<br>Back | **UI-47**<br>Toolbar Back/Cancel returns without changing state. | Follow-up state review for one identified saved task.<br>Pattern: P-09 | C baseline — presentation specified | **Source result:** Return unchanged or leave the parent completion choice unstaged.<br>**Source continuation / error:** No premature closure from a draft visit. |

## Audit boundary

The enumerated count is mechanically checked against the extracted source IDs, with no missing, extra or duplicate action IDs. It is not evidence that the proposed controls have passed usability or device tests. Source disagreements are deliberately visible; an inactive F alternative is a disposition, not an implemented feature. The main document records the remaining owner/security/platform validation gates.
